Tong et al. 2021b
)
under Gaussian noise. Top two figures:
n=2.5rd_{1}
; bottom two figures:
n=5rd_{1}
.

The second set of simulation is to test the convergence performance of RsGrad and ScaledSM under heavy-tailed noise. For simplicity, the noise is sampled independently from a Student’s
t
-distribution with
{\rm d.f.}

\nu=2
, which has a finite first (absolute) moment and an infinite second moment. Other parameters are selected the same as in the Gaussian case. Figure
2
presents the convergence performance showing that both RsGrad (either absolute loss or Huber loss) and ScaledSM are robust to heavy-tailed noise.
Similarly, we can observe dual-phase convergence of RsGrad whose second phase iterations deliver a more accurate estimate than ScaledSM, especially when SNR is large.

)
under Student’s
t
-distribution with d.f.

. Top two figures:
n=2.5rd_{1}
; bottom two figures:
n=5rd_{1}
.

Statistical accuracy

We now compare the statistical accuracy of the final estimator output by RsGrad, ScaledSM and those by convex approaches including the nuclear norm penalized
(
Elsener and van de Geer 2018
)
absolute loss (convex
\ell_{1}
), Huber loss (convex Huber) and the square loss (
\ell_{2}
-loss) by seminal work
Candes and Plan 2011
. Functions from Matlab library cvx
(
Grant and Boyd 2014
)
are borrowed to implement the convex methods for convex
\ell_{1}
, convex Huber and
\ell_{2}
-loss. For each setting, every method is repeated for
10
times and the box-plots of error rates are presented.

The parameter settings are similar as above where both the Gaussian noise and Student’s
t
-distribution are experimented. The comparison of statistical accuracy under Gaussian noise is displayed in Figure
3
. The top two plots suggest that all the convex methods and ScaledSM performs poorly when sample size is small (
n=2.5rd_{1}
). On the other hand, ScaledSM becomes much better when sample size is large but still under-performs RsGrad, as predicted by our theory. Similar results are also observed when noise has a Students’
t
-distribution with d.f.
\nu=2
. See Figure
4
.

SNR=40

(

)
and convex
\ell_{2}
loss
(

)
under Gaussian noise. Top two figures:

; bottom two figures:

)

-distribution noise with d.f.

. Top two figures:

n=5rd_{1}
.

From Figure
3
and Figure
4
, we may conclude that nonconvex models are statistically more accurate in practice for both Gaussian noise and heavy-tailed noise, though convex approaches are proven statistically optimal in theory. Moreover, convex approaches are computationally much more demanding. For instance, in the case
n=5rd_{1}
, convex
\ell_{1}
, convex Huber, convex
\ell_{2}
need more than 1.5h, 1.5h, 3h to complete the ten repetitions, respectively. In sharp contrast, RsGrad and ScaledSM only take around ten minutes. When it comes to an even larger sample size like
n=10d_{1}r
, the Matlab package on our computing platform needs 3h and 6h to complete the simulations (10 repetitions) for convex
\ell_{1}
and convex Huber, respectively, whereas RsGrad and ScaledSM each takes about 20 minutes to do the job.

7.2
Low-rank Tensor Estimation

This section presents the results of numerical experiments on RsGrad for low-rank tensor estimation. The stepsizes schedule, relative error and SNR are similar to the matrix simulations. For simplicity, the tensor size is fixed at
30\times 30\times 30
with Tucker rank
(2,2,2)
.

Convergence dynamics

RsGrad is compared with the
\ell_{2}
-loss Riemannian gradient algorithm, RGrad
(
Cai et al. 2020
)
under both Gaussian and heavy-tailed noise. The results are displayed in Figure
5
. Under Gaussian noise, RsGrad slightly under-performs RGrad. However, when the noise has a Student’s t-distribution with d.f.
\nu=2.1
, RsGrad significantly outperforms RGrad. Clearly, the dual-phase convergence of RsGrad is observed confirming our theory.

.

. The simulation results under small and large SNRs are presented in Figure
6
. It shows that, under heavy-tailed noise, our initialization method attains a smaller relative error.

Appendix A
Proofs for main results

A.1
Proof of Proposition
1

For convenience, denote
d_{l}:=(1-0.04\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{-2})^{l}\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and proof of phase one is equivalent to verification of
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l+1}
when
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\tau_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
.

Prove by induction and when
l=0
,
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{0}
is obvious. Suppose we already have
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l}
and we are going to prove
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l+1}
.

Notice that
{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}=\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l}))
and we use Lemma
16
to bound the distance between
{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}
and the ground truth matrix
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}
. First consider
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
,

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}&=\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(11)

The second term can be bounded in the way of

\begin{split}\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle&=\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\rangle-\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle\\
&\geq(f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))-\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}(\mathbf{G}_{l})\rangle,\\
\end{split}

where the last inequality is from definition of sub-gradient. Note that

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{\ast},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\|({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{l}{\mathbf{U}}_{l}^{\top})({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*})({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{V}}_{l}{\mathbf{V}}_{l}^{\top})\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},r}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}{\sigma_{r}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{\ast}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

which uses
\|{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{l}{\mathbf{U}}_{l}^{\top}\|\leq\frac{4\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}}
(see Lemma
16
) and
\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
. The above two equations lead to

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle\geq(f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))-4\frac{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}{\sigma_{r}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{\ast}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Together with
\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{2}\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq\sqrt{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
(see Lemma
15
),
f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{8}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}\sigma_{r}
, Equation
11
could be bounded in the way of

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\eta_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+2\eta_{l}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}.

By induction, one already has
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq(1-0.04\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}/L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2})^{l}\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}=:d_{l}
and
\eta_{l}
satisfies
\frac{1}{5}d_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{-2}\leq\eta_{l}=(1-0.04\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}/L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2})^{l}\cdot\eta_{0}\leq\frac{3}{10}d_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{-2}
, which implies

\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{3}{25}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})d_{l}^{2}.

(12)

It shows
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}<\sigma_{r}/4
and then by Lemma
16
, one could bound
\|\operatorname{SVD}_{r}(\mathbf{M}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l}))-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
:

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}&=\|{\rm SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l}))-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{50}{\sigma_{r}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3},\end{split}

(13)

Insert Equation
12
into Equation
13
:

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{3}{25}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})d_{l}^{2}+\frac{50}{\sigma_{r}}(1-\frac{3}{25}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}}d_{l}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{2}{25}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})d_{l}^{2},

which uses
\frac{50}{\sigma_{r}}d_{l}\leq\frac{50}{\sigma_{r}}d_{0}\leq\frac{1}{25}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}
. It shows

\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l+1}=(1-0.04\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}/L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2})^{l+1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Hence, analysis of phase one convergence has been finished. Then consider the second phase,
\tau_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}<\tau_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
. The derivation is similar:

\displaystyle{~~~~}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l})\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}(f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))+2\eta_{l}\frac{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{\sigma_{r}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{3}+2\eta_{l}^{2}\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\eta_{l}\frac{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{\sigma_{r}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}+2\eta_{l}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{7}{4}\eta_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\eta_{l}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from the condition
f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
,
\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and the third inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{8}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}\sigma_{r}
.
Take stepsize
\frac{1}{8}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}\leq\eta_{l}\leq\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}
and one has

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{1}{8}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

This implies
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}<\sigma_{r}/4
and then similarly, use Lemma
16
to cope with the matrix perturbation,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\rm SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l}))-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{50}{\sigma_{r}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{8}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{50}{\sigma_{r}}(1-\frac{1}{8}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})^{3/2}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{16}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

which uses the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{800}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}\sigma_{r}
. It verifies

\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq(1-\frac{1}{32}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|\mathbf{M}_{l}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},

which finishes the proof.

A.2
Proof of tensor case Proposition
2

For convenience, denote
d_{l}:=(1-\frac{1}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})^{l}\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and proof of phase one is equivalent to verification of
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l+1}
when
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\tau_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
.

Prove by induction and when
l=0
,
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{0}
is obvious. Suppose we already have
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l}
and we are going to prove
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l+1}
.

Notice that
{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}=\text{HOSVD}_{\mathbf{r}}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l}))
and we use Lemma
16
to bound the distance between singular value truncated matrix and the ground truth matrix
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}
. First consider
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
,

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}&=\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(14)

The second term can be bounded in the way of

\begin{split}\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle&=\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\rangle-\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle\\
&\geq(f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))-\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}(\mathbf{G}_{l})\rangle,\\
\end{split}

where the last inequality is from definition of sub-gradient. Note that the second term could be bounded with

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{\ast},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle\leq\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq\frac{8m(m+3)L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{\ast}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

which uses Lemma
22
and
\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
. The above two equations lead to

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\rangle\geq(f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))-8m(m+3)\frac{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{\ast}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Together with
\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{m+1}\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}\leq\sqrt{m+1}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
(see Lemma
21
),
f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{16}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}{m(m+3)L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}}\underline{\lambda}
, Equation
14
becomes

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\eta_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+(m+1)\eta_{l}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}.

By induction, one already has
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq(1-\frac{1}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})^{l}\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}=:d_{l}
. Also, stepsize
\eta_{l}
satisfies
\frac{1}{4(m+1)}d_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{-2}\leq\eta_{l}=(1-\frac{1}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})^{l}\cdot\eta_{0}\leq\frac{3}{4(m+1)}d_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{-2}
and it implies

\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{3}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})d_{l}^{2}.

(15)

It shows
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}<\underline{\lambda}/4
and then by Lemma B.2 of
Cai et al. 2021a
, one could bound
\|\operatorname{HOSVD}_{{\mathbf{r}}}(\mathbf{M}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l}))-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
:

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}&=\|{\rm HOSVD}_{{\mathbf{r}}}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l}))-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{1}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3},\end{split}

(16)

Insert Equation
15
into Equation
16
:

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{3}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})d_{l}^{2}+\frac{c_{1}}{\underline{\lambda}}(1-\frac{3}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}}d_{l}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{8(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})d_{l}^{2},

which uses
\frac{c_{1}}{\underline{\lambda}}d_{l}\leq\frac{c_{1}}{\underline{\lambda}}d_{0}\leq\frac{1}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}\underline{\lambda}
. Take the square root and then we have

\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq d_{l+1}=(1-\frac{1}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}^{2}})^{l+1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Hence, analysis of phase one convergence has been finished. Then consider the second phase,
\tau_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}<\tau_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
. The derivation is similar:

\displaystyle{~~~~}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\langle{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l})\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}(f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))+16\eta_{l}\frac{m(m+3)L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{3}+(m+1)\eta_{l}^{2}\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+16\eta_{l}\frac{m(m+3)L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}+(m+1)\eta_{l}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{l}\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(m+1)\eta_{l}^{2}L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from the condition
f({\mathbf{M}}_{l})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
,
\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and the third inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{16m(m+3)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}\underline{\lambda}
.
Take stepsize
\frac{1}{4(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}\leq\eta_{l}\leq\frac{3}{4(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}
and one has

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{3}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

This implies
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}<\underline{\lambda}/4
and then similarly, use Lemma B.2
Cai et al. 2021a
to cope with the tensor perturbation,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\rm HOSVD}_{{\mathbf{r}}}({\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta_{l}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\mathbf{G}_{l}))-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{1}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-\eta{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{3}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{1}}{\underline{\lambda}}(1-\frac{3}{16(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})^{3/2}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{1}{8(m+1)}\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

which uses the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}_{0}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{16c_{1}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}\underline{\lambda}
. It verifies

\|{\mathbf{M}}_{l+1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq(1-\frac{1}{16(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}}{L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}^{2}})\|\mathbf{M}_{l}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

We finish the proof.

Appendix B
Proofs for Applications

B.1
Proof of absolute loss with Gaussian noise Lemma
1

Proof.

First consider upper bound of sug-gradient
{\mathbf{G}}\in f({\mathbf{M}})
, where matrix
{\mathbf{M}}
has rank at most
r
. Note that we have,

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})

\displaystyle=\big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}|Y_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}),{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|-\sum_{i=1}^{n}|Y_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|\big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\langle\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}),{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|\leq 2n\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r},

where the last inequality holds with probability exceeding
1-4\exp(-cd_{1}r)
(see Corollary
2
). On the other hand, by definition of sub-gradient, we have
f({\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}
and together with the above equation, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq 2n
. It implies
L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=2n
.

Next consider the lower bound for
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
. Its expectation
{\mathbb{E}}[f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})]
is calculated in Lemma
17
. We shall proceed assuming the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{r}}|f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\}
holds and specifically Theorem
10
proves
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}
holds with probability exceeding
1-\exp(-cd_{1}r)
.

We discuss the two phases respectively, namely, phase one when
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sigma
and phase two when
C\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}\sigma\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sigma
.

Case 1:

When
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\geq\sigma
, we have

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]-C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=n\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq n\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}+1}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}-C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12}n\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where the penultimate line is from
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\geq\sigma
and the last line uses
n\geq Cd_{1}r
for some large absolute constant
C>0
. It verifies
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=\frac{1}{12}n
.

Case 2:

When
C\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}\sigma\leq\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}<\sigma
, we have

\begin{split}f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})&\geq\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]-C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&=n\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&\geq\frac{1}{6}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-C_{2}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&\geq\frac{1}{12}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},\end{split}

where the penultimate line uses
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}<\sigma
and the last line is from
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}\sigma
. It shows
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=\frac{1}{12}\frac{n}{\sigma}
.

Finally, from the following Lemma
7
, we see that
L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq C_{4}n\sigma^{-1}
. And this finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 7

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
have rank at most
r
and satisfy
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}r}{n}}\sigma
. Let
{\mathbf{G}}\in\partial f({\mathbf{M}})
be the sub-gradient. Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{r}}|f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\}
, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq Cn\sigma^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
for some absolute constant
C>0
.

Proof.

Take
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
, where
\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
is the best rank
r
approximation of
{\mathbf{G}}
. So
\text{rank}({\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}_{1})\leq r
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/\pi}n\frac{\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}{\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/\pi}n\frac{\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}{\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{n}{\sigma}\left(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right),

where the first equality comes from Lemma
17
. And from Theorem
10
, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}(2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+(2+C_{1})\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality is from the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}r}{n}}\sigma
. Since
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
, we have

\begin{split}f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))&-f({\mathbf{M}})\\
&\leq\frac{\sigma}{4n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,r}}^{2}+(1+C_{1}/2)\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,r}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(17)

On the other hand, by the definition of sub-gradient, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\frac{\sigma}{2n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}.

(18)

Combine Equation
17
with Equation
18
and by solving the quadratic inequality we get

\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq Cn\sigma^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

∎

B.2
Proof of absolute value loss with heavy-tailed noise Lemma
2

The
L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=2n
proof is the same as the one given in Section
B.1
. Now we focus on lower bound of
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
.

First consider phase one when
\|{\mathbf{X}}-{\mathbf{X}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 30{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=30\gamma
. Use triangle inequality and then get

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|-\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\xi_{i}|\geq\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|-2\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}.

(19)

Corollary
2
proves
\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|\geq\sqrt{1/2\pi}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and Lemma
18
proves with probability over
1-{}^{*}\!c_{1}n^{-\min\{1,\varepsilon\}}
,
\frac{1}{n}\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=3\gamma
. Then combined with
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 30\gamma
, Equation
19
could be further bounded with

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})\geq\sqrt{1/2\pi}n\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}-6n\gamma\geq\frac{n}{6}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},

which verifies
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=\frac{n}{6}
.

Then consider the second phase when
C_{2}\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{0}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}<30\gamma
. To bound expectation of
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
, we first consider the conditional expectation
{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
. Notice for all
t_{0}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}|\xi-t_{0}|

\displaystyle=\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(s-t_{0})dH_{\xi}(s)+\int_{s<t_{0}}(t_{0}-s)dH_{\xi}(s)

\displaystyle=2\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(s-t_{0})dH_{\xi}(s)+\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}(t_{0}-s)dH_{\xi}(s)

\displaystyle=2\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(1-H_{\xi}(s))ds+t_{0}-\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}sdH_{\xi}(s).

Set
t_{0}=\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle
and define
g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}):={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{i}}|\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle|
. It implies

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})=2\int_{s\geq\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}(1-H_{\xi}(s))ds+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle-\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}sdH_{\xi}(s).

This leads to

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=2\int_{0}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}H_{\xi}(s)ds-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle.

(20)

Note that
\langle\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\rangle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})
and denote
z:=\langle\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\rangle
. Let
f_{z}(\cdot)
be the density of
z
. Take expectation of
z
on both sides of Equation
20

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[g_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(\mathbf{M})-g_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=2\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t}\left(H_{\xi}(s)-0.5\right)f_{z}(t)\,ds\,dz

\displaystyle=2\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s}h_{\xi}(w)f_{z}(t)\,dw\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\geq 2\int_{-\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}^{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\int_{0}^{t}f_{z}(t)b_{0}^{-1}s\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle=b_{0}^{-1}\int_{-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}^{\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}t^{2}f_{z}(t)\,dt=b_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}e^{-t^{2}/2}\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the first inequality follows from Assumption
2
and the last is from
\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}e^{-t^{2}/2}\,dt\geq 1/6
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\frac{n}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Invoke Theorem
10
,

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}[f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})]-C\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-C\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{12b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{0}
. This proves
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=\frac{n}{12b_{0}}
.

Finally, from the following lemma, we see that
L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}
.
And this finishes the proof.

Lemma 8

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be rank at most
r
matrix such that
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}r}{n}}b_{1}
. Let
{\mathbf{G}}\in\partial f({\mathbf{M}})
be the sub-gradient. Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{r}}|f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\}
, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
for some absolute constant
C_{3}>0
.

Proof.

Take
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
, where
\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
is the best rank
r
approximation of
{\mathbf{G}}
and then
\text{rank}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})\leq 2r
. We finish the proof via combining lower bound and upper bound of
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
. First consider
{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})
.
Use the same notation as the above proof and similarly,

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle 2\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}\int_{0}^{\xi}h_{\xi}(x)\,dx\,d\xi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2b_{1}^{-1}\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}\xi\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle b_{1}^{-1}\left[(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)^{2}-(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)^{2}\right],

where the inequality follows from the upper bound for
h_{\xi}(x)
. Then take expectation on each side and sum up over
i
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}[g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nb_{1}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)^{2}-(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nb_{1}^{-1}\left[\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

Bound
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
using Corollary
1
:

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}(2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+(2+C_{3})nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+2C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality is from the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}r}{n}}b_{1}
. Since
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
, it becomes

\begin{split}f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))&-f({\mathbf{M}})\\
&\leq\frac{b_{1}}{4n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,r}}^{2}+(1+C_{1}/2)\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,r}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{b_{1}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(21)

On the other hand, by the definition of sub-gradient,
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
has lower bound

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}.

(22)

Combine Equation
21
with Equation
22
and then solve the quadratic inequality which leads to

\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

∎

B.3
Proof of Huber loss with heavy-tailed noise Lemma
3

First consider upper bound of sug-gradient
{\mathbf{G}}\in f({\mathbf{M}})
, where matrix
{\mathbf{M}}
has rank at most
r
. We have

\displaystyle|f({\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})\rangle)-\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}2\delta|\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})\rangle\leq 4\delta n\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r},

where the last line comes from Corollary
2
. Note that it implies
L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=4\delta n
and the reason has been stated in Section
B.1
.

Then consider the lower bound. Use the inequality
2\delta|x|-\delta^{2}\leq\rho_{H,\delta}(x)\leq 2\delta|x|
and then we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)-\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i})

\displaystyle\geq 2\delta\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle|-|\xi_{i}|)-n\delta^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\delta\sqrt{2/\pi}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}-4\delta\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}-n\delta^{2},

where the last inequality comes from Corollary
2
. Also, Lemma
18
proves with high probability
\frac{1}{n}\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=3\gamma
, which implies

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\delta\sqrt{2/\pi}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}-12\delta n\gamma-n\delta^{2}.

Therefore when
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 24\gamma+2\delta
, we have
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\frac{\delta n}{4}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
. It shows with
\tau_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=24\gamma+2\delta
we have
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=\delta n/4
.

Now consider the second phase. First take expectation against
\xi
and define
g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}):={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}[\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)]
. Denote
z=\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle
. Then with a little abuse of notation, define

\displaystyle g(z)

\displaystyle:=2\delta\int_{\delta+z}^{+\infty}(x-z)\,dH_{\xi}(x)+2\delta\int_{-\infty}^{z-\delta}(z-x)dH_{\xi}(x)+\int_{z-\delta}^{z+\delta}\left(z-x\right)^{2}dH_{\xi}(x)

(23)

\displaystyle=g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}).

(24)

Then simple computation shows

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(z)

\displaystyle=2\int_{z-\delta}^{z+\delta}H_{\xi}(x)dx-2\delta,

\displaystyle g^{\prime\prime}(z)

\displaystyle=2(H_{\xi}(z+\delta)-H_{\xi}(z-\delta)).

Assumption
3
guarantees
g^{\prime}(0)=0
. Therefore the Taylor expansion at
0
is

\displaystyle g(z)=g(0)+2\int_{0}^{z}(H_{\xi}(y+\delta)-H_{\xi}(y-\delta))y\,dy.

Notice
z=\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle\sim N(0,\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2})
and denote its density by
f_{z}(\cdot)
. Take expectation w.r.t.
z
and we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}g(z)-{\mathbb{E}}g(0)

\displaystyle=2\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{t}(H_{\xi}(y+\delta)-H_{\xi}(y-\delta))yf_{z}(t)\,dy\,dt

\displaystyle\geq 2\int_{-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}^{\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}\int_{0}^{t}f_{z}(t)(H_{\xi}(y+\delta)-H_{\xi}(y-\delta))y\,dy\,dt

\displaystyle\geq 4\delta b_{0}^{-1}\int_{-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}^{\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}t^{2}f_{z}(t)\,dt=4\delta b_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp(-t^{2}/2)\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2}{3}\delta b_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality uses
H_{\xi}(y+\delta)-H_{\xi}(y-\delta)\geq 2\delta b_{0}^{-1}
when
y\leq\tau_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}
. And this implies

{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=n\left({\mathbb{E}}g(z)-{\mathbb{E}}g(0)\right)\geq 2\delta nb_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And together with Theorem
10
, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}[f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})]-(C_{2}+C_{3}\delta)\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

And thus when
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq b_{0}\frac{C_{2}+C_{3}\delta}{\delta}\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}
, we have

f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\frac{1}{3}\delta nb_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

It proves with
\tau_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=b_{0}\frac{C_{2}+C_{3}\delta}{\delta}\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}
it has
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=\frac{1}{3}\delta nb_{0}^{-1}
.

And the bound for
L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}
is given as follows.

Lemma 9

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be rank at most
r
matrix and satisfy
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq c\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{0}
. Let
{\mathbf{G}}\in\partial f({\mathbf{M}})
be the sub-gradient. Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{r}}|f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\}
, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq C_{\delta}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
for
C_{\delta}=C_{1}+C_{2}\delta
and some absolute constant
C_{1},C_{2}>0
.

Proof.

Take
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{4n\delta}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
. First consider
{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})
. Continue from Equation
23
,

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)\right]

\displaystyle=2\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}[H_{\xi}(y+\delta)-H_{\xi}(y-\delta)]y\,dy

\displaystyle\leq 4\delta b_{1}^{-1}\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}y\,dy

\displaystyle=2\delta b_{1}^{-1}[\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle].

Take expectation on each side and sum up ove
i
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})

\displaystyle=n\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\rho_{H,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta nb_{1}^{-1}[\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}].

Then invoke Crorllary
1
and bound
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
:

\displaystyle{~~~~}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{\delta}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\delta nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+C_{\delta}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+c_{\delta}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+C_{\delta}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{1}
. Insert
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{4n\delta}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
and then it is

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})\leq\frac{b_{1}}{8n\delta}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}+\frac{c_{\delta}}{4\delta}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\frac{C_{\delta}n}{b_{1}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(25)

On the other hand, from the definition of the sub-gradient, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})=f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{4n\delta}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\frac{b_{1}}{4n\delta}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}.

(26)

Combine Equation
25
with Equation
26
and then the quadratic inequality of
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}
gives

\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq C_{\delta}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

∎

B.4
Proof of quantile loss with heavy-tailed noise Lemma
4

Notice that quantile loss function
\rho_{Q,\delta}(\cdot)
is Lipschitz continuous with
\max\{\delta,1-\delta\}
. First consider upper bound of sug-gradient
{\mathbf{G}}\in f({\mathbf{M}})
, where matrix
{\mathbf{M}}
has rank at most
r
. We have

\displaystyle|f({\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}+\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})\rangle)-\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max\{\delta,1-\delta\}|\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq 2\max\{\delta,1-\delta\}n\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r},

where the last line comes from Corollary
2
. Note that it verifies
L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=2\max\{\delta,1-\delta\}n
and its proof is same as Section
B.1
.

Then consider the lower bound. Notice that quantile loss function satisfies triangle inequality,
\rho_{Q,\delta}(x_{1}+x_{2})\leq\rho_{Q,\delta}(x_{1})+\rho_{Q,\delta}(x_{2})
and then it has

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)-\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi_{i})

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\rho_{Q,\delta}(-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)-\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi_{i})-\rho_{Q,\delta}(-\xi_{i})

\displaystyle\geq\min\{\delta,1-\delta\}\sqrt{1/2\pi}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1},

where the last inequality comes from empirical process results Corollary
2
. Lemma
18
proves with probability over
1-{}^{*}\!c_{1}n^{-\min\{1,\varepsilon\}}
,
\frac{1}{n}\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}\leq 3\gamma
. Then it leads to

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\min\{\delta,1-\delta\}\sqrt{1/2\pi}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}-3n\gamma.

Therefore when
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 15\gamma\max\{\frac{1}{\delta},\frac{1}{1-\delta}\}
, it has

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\frac{1}{6}\min\{\delta,1-\delta\}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},

which proves with
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=\frac{1}{6}\min\{\delta,1-\delta\}n
.

Then consider the second phase where
c\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{0}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 15\gamma\max\{\frac{1}{\delta},\frac{1}{1-\delta}\}
. Notice for all
t_{0}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi-t_{0})

\displaystyle=\delta\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(s-t_{0})\,dH_{\xi}(s)+(1-\delta)\int_{s<t_{0}}(t_{0}-s)\,dH_{\xi}(s)

\displaystyle=\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(s-t_{0})\,dH_{\xi}(s)+(1-\delta)\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}(t_{0}-s)\,dH_{\xi}(s)

\displaystyle=\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(1-H_{\xi}(s))\,ds+(1-\delta)t_{0}-(1-\delta)\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}s\,dH_{\xi}(s).

Set
t_{0}=\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle
and define
g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}):={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{i}}\rho_{Q,\delta}(\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)
. It implies

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})=\int_{s\geq\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}(1-H_{\xi}(s))\,ds+(1-\delta)\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle-(1-\delta)\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}s\,dH_{\xi}(s).

This leads to

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=\int_{0}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}H_{\xi}(s)\,ds-\delta\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle.

(27)

Note that
\langle\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\rangle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2})
and denote
z:=\langle\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\rangle
. Let
f_{z}(\cdot)
be the density of
z
. Take expectation of
z
on both sides of Equation
27

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[g_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(\mathbf{M})-g_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t}\left(H_{\xi}(s)-\delta\right)f_{z}(t)\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s}h_{\xi}(w)f_{z}(t)\,dw\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\geq b_{0}^{-1}\int_{-\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}^{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\int_{0}^{t}f_{z}(t)s\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle=b_{0}^{-1}\int_{-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}^{\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}t^{2}f_{z}(t)\,dt=b_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}e^{-t^{2}/2}\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the first inequality follows from Assumption
4
and the last line is from
\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}e^{-t^{2}/2}dt\geq 1/6
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}^{*})\geq\frac{n}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Invoke Theorem
10
,

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})-C\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-C\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{12b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{X}}-{\mathbf{X}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq c\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{0}
. This proves the lower bound in the second phase and shows
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=\frac{n}{12b_{0}}
.

Finally, from the following lemma, we see that
L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq C_{2}nb_{1}^{-1}
. And this finished the proof of the lemma.

Lemma 10

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
be at most rank
r
matrix such that
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{d_{1}r/n}b_{1}
. Let
{\mathbf{G}}\in\partial f({\mathbf{M}})
be the sub-gradient. Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{r}}|f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}\}
,, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq Cnb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
for some absolute constant
C>0
.

Proof.

Take
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
, where
\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
is the best rank
r
approximation of
{\mathbf{G}}
and then
\text{rank}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})\leq 2r
. First consider
{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})
.
Use the same notation as in the above proof and similarly,

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}\int_{0}^{\xi}h_{\xi}(x)\,dx\,d\xi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{1}^{-1}\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}\xi\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2b_{1}}\left[(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)^{2}-(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)^{2}\right],

where the inequality follows from the upper bound for
h_{\xi}(x)\leq b_{1}^{-1}
. Then take expectation on each side and sum up:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}[g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2b_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)^{2}-(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2b_{1}}\left[\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

Bound
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
using Corollary
1
:

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\sqrt{nd_{1}r}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}(2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2b_{1}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+(1+C_{1})nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+2C_{1}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality is from the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq c\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}r}{n}}b_{1}
. Since
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}})
, it becomes

\begin{split}f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))&-f({\mathbf{M}})\\
&\leq\frac{b_{1}}{8n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,r}}^{2}+(1/2+C_{1}/2)\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,r}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{b_{1}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(28)

On the other hand, by the definition of sub-gradient,
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
has lower bound

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\text{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{G}}))-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{2n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}^{2}.

(29)

Combine Equation
28
, Equation
29
and then solve the quadratic inequality which leads to

\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},r}\leq Cnb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

∎

B.5
Proof of tensor case, absolute loss with Gaussian noise Lemma
5

Proof.

We shall use
{\mathbb{O}}_{d,r}:=\{{\mathbf{U}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times r}:\,{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}={\mathbf{I}}_{r}\}
to refer to set of all
d
-by-
r
matrices with orthonormal columns and
{\mathbb{O}}_{r}
to be set of dimension
r
orthonormal matrices.

Note that when
n\geq C(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j})
, with probability exceeding
1-4\exp(-c(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}))
,
L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=2n
. The reason is similar to the one presented in Section
B.1
and here we use Gaussian process Corollary
4
to cope with tensor.

Next consider the lower bound for
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
. Its expectation
{\mathbb{E}}[f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})]
is calculated in Lemma
17
. We shall proceed assuming the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{\mathbf{r}}}|f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\}
holds and specifically Theorem
11
proves
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}
holds with probability exceeding
1-\exp(-c(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j}))
.

We discuss the two phases respectively, namely, phase one when
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sigma
and phase two when
C\sqrt{(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})/n}\sigma\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sigma
.

Case 1:

When
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\geq\sigma
, we have

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]-C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=n\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq n\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}+1}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}-C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{12}n\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where the penultimate line is from
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\geq\sigma
and the last line uses
n\geq C(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})
for some large absolute constant
C>0
. It verifies
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=\frac{1}{12}n
.

Case 2:

When
C\sqrt{(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})/n}\sigma\leq\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}<\sigma
, we have

\begin{split}f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})&\geq\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]-C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&=n\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&\geq\frac{1}{6}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-C_{2}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&\geq\frac{1}{12}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},\end{split}

where the penultimate line uses
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}<\sigma
and the last line is from
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C\sqrt{(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})/n}\sigma
. It shows
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=\frac{1}{12}\frac{n}{\sigma}
.

Finally, from the following Lemma
11
, we see that
L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq C_{4}n\sigma^{-1}
. And this finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 11

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let tensor
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
have rank at most
{\mathbf{r}}
and satisfy
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{n^{-1}(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\sigma
. Let
{\mathbf{G}}\in\partial f({\mathbf{M}})
be the sub-gradient. Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{\mathbf{r}},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{2{\mathbf{r}}}}|f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\}
, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq Cn\sigma^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
for some absolute constant
C>0
.

Proof.

There exists orthonormal matrices
{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{1},2r_{1}},\cdots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{m},2r_{m}}
such that
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}=\|{\mathbf{G}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}
and denote
\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}}:={\mathbf{G}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}
. Take
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}}
. So
\text{rank}({\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}})\leq 2{\mathbf{r}}
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/\pi}n\frac{\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}{\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/\pi}n\frac{\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}{\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/\pi}\frac{n}{\sigma}\left(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right),

where the first equality comes from Lemma
17
. And from Corollary
3
, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}(2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+(2+C_{1})\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality is from the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{\frac{2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j}}{n}}\sigma
. Since
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}}
, we have

\begin{split}f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}})&-f({\mathbf{M}})\\
&\leq\frac{\sigma}{4n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}^{2}+(1+C_{1}/2)\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{\sigma}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(30)

On the other hand, by the definition of sub-gradient, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{\sigma}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}})-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\frac{\sigma}{2n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}^{2}.

(31)

Combine Equation
30
with Equation
31
and by solving the quadratic inequality we get

\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq Cn\sigma^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

∎

B.6
Proof of tensor case, absolute value loss with heavy-tailed noise Lemma
6

The
L_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=2n
proof is the same as the one given in Section
B.5
. Now we focus on lower bound of
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
.

First consider phase one when
\|{\mathbf{X}}-{\mathbf{X}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 30{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=30\gamma
. Use triangle inequality and then get

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|-\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\xi_{i}|\geq\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|-2\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}.

(32)

Corollary
4
proves
\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\langle{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*},{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\rangle|\geq\sqrt{1/2\pi}n\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
and Lemma
18
proves with probability over
1-{}^{*}\!c_{1}n^{-\min\{1,\varepsilon\}}
,
\frac{1}{n}\|\boldsymbol{\xi}\|_{1}\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=3\gamma
. Then combined with
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 30\gamma
, Equation
32
becomes

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{M})-f(\mathbf{M}^{*})\geq\sqrt{1/2\pi}n\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}-6n\gamma\geq\frac{n}{6}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},

which verifies
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny comp}}=\frac{n}{6}
.

Then consider the second phase when
C_{2}\sqrt{(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})/n}b_{0}\leq\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}<30\gamma
. To bound expectation of
f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
, we first consider the conditional expectation
{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})
. Notice for all
t_{0}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}|\xi-t_{0}|

\displaystyle=\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(s-t_{0})dH_{\xi}(s)+\int_{s<t_{0}}(t_{0}-s)dH_{\xi}(s)

\displaystyle=2\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(s-t_{0})dH_{\xi}(s)+\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}(t_{0}-s)dH_{\xi}(s)

\displaystyle=2\int_{s\geq t_{0}}(1-H_{\xi}(s))ds+t_{0}-\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}sdH_{\xi}(s).

Set
t_{0}=\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle
and define
g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}):={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{i}}|\xi_{i}-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle|
. It implies

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})=2\int_{s\geq\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}(1-H_{\xi}(s))ds+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle-\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}sdH_{\xi}(s).

This leads to

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=2\int_{0}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}H_{\xi}(s)ds-\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle.

(33)

Note that
\langle\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\rangle
follows Gaussian distribution with mean zero variance
\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
and denote it as
z:=\langle\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\rangle
. Let
f_{z}(\cdot)
be the density of
z
. Take expectation of
z
on both sides of Equation
33

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[g_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(\mathbf{M})-g_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(\mathbf{M}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=2\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t}\left(H_{\xi}(s)-0.5\right)f_{z}(t)\,ds\,dz

\displaystyle=2\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s}h_{\xi}(w)f_{z}(t)\,dw\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\geq 2\int_{-\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}^{\|\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\int_{0}^{t}f_{z}(t)b_{0}^{-1}s\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle=b_{0}^{-1}\int_{-\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}^{\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}t^{2}f_{z}(t)\,dt=b_{0}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}e^{-t^{2}/2}\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the first inequality follows from Assumption
2
and the last is from
\int_{-1}^{1}t^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}e^{-t^{2}/2}\,dt\geq 1/6
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}[g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}^{*})]\geq\frac{n}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Invoke Theorem
11
,

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}[f({\mathbf{M}})-f({\mathbf{M}}^{*})]-C\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{6b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-C\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{12b_{0}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})/n}b_{0}
. This proves
\mu_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}=\frac{n}{12b_{0}}
.

Finally, from the following lemma, we see that
L_{\textsf{\tiny stat}}\leq C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}
.
And this finishes the proof.

Lemma 12

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
be rank at most
{\mathbf{r}}
tensor such that
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{n^{-1}(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}b_{1}
. Let
{\mathbf{G}}\in\partial f({\mathbf{M}})
be the sub-gradient. Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\{\sup_{{\mathbf{M}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{\mathbf{r}},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{2{\mathbf{r}}}}|f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})-{\mathbb{E}}(f({\mathbf{M}}+{\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}}))|\cdot\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}\}
, we have
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
for some absolute constant
C_{3}>0
.

Proof.

There exists orthonormal matrices
{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{1},2r_{1}},\cdots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{m},2r_{m}}
such that
\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}=\|{\mathbf{G}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}
and denote
\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}}:={\mathbf{G}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}
. Take
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}}
and then
\text{rank}({\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}})\leq 2{\mathbf{r}}
. We finish the proof via combining lower bound and upper bound of
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
. First consider
{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})
.
Use the same notation as the above proof and similarly,

\displaystyle g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle 2\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}\int_{0}^{\xi}h_{\xi}(x)\,dx\,d\xi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2b_{1}^{-1}\int_{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle}^{\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle}\xi\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle b_{1}^{-1}\left[(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)^{2}-(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)^{2}\right],

where the inequality follows from the upper bound for
h_{\xi}(x)
. Then take expectation of
{\mathbf{X}}_{i}
on each side and sum up over
i
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}[g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-g_{{\mathbf{X}}_{i}}({\mathbf{M}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nb_{1}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\rangle)^{2}-(\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\rangle)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nb_{1}^{-1}\left[\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

Then bound
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
with empirical process Corollary
3
:

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{1}\sqrt{n(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}(\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathbf{M}})+C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}(2\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+(2+C_{3})nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{M}}_{1}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+2C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality is from the condition
\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{n^{-1}(2^{m}\cdot r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+2\sum_{j=1}^{m}d_{j}r_{j})}b_{1}
. Since
{\mathbf{M}}_{1}={\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}}
, it becomes

\begin{split}f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}})&-f({\mathbf{M}})\\
&\leq\frac{b_{1}}{4n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}^{2}+(1+C_{1}/2)\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}+2C_{1}\frac{n}{b_{1}}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(34)

On the other hand, by the definition of sub-gradient,
f({\mathbf{M}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{M}})
has lower bound

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{M}}+\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\tilde{{\mathbf{G}}})-f({\mathbf{M}})\geq\frac{b_{1}}{2n}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}^{2}.

(35)

Combine Equation
34
with Equation
35
and then solve the quadratic inequality which leads to

\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq C_{3}nb_{1}^{-1}\|{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

∎

Appendix C
Proof of Initialization Theorem
5

Notice that

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}{\mathbf{X}}_{i}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|=
